COMPONENTS / HUB75-FRAME-COUPLER-CORNER-RIGHT-TUBE-RECEIVER

HUB75 Frame Coupler — Corner Right Tube Receiver

Configured right Corner coupler with one female receiver for a male tube clamp

Built in SolidCraft 0.50.0 238 commands HUB75 Frame Coupler · all variants

Model preview

Alternate right Corner tube interface on the shared Small/Medium/Large base, retaining the right-corner panel-pin suppression behavior before the mirrored common clearances and final receiving dovetail geometry.

Key dimensions: Small / Medium / Large; Medium default; local receiver center -7 mm; Ø10.5 tube clearance; 0.2 mm dovetail fit

Files

Source stays on the source branch; generated CAD artifacts stay on the durable build branch.

SCPF source Command JSON ↗ SolidCraft .sc ↗ View STL on GitHub ↗ Download STL STEP ↗ model.yml ci.yml

Generated files are not republished in gh-pages; these links point to the accepted build scope.

Construction stages

  • Reuse the shared configured right Corner history
  • Apply the shared insertion and tube clearances
  • Add the 16 mm receiver support
  • Cut the source female dovetail groove and closed stop
